Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the microevolution framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 28. The resulting operator achieves bound O(898/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_28(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 28. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^898(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.3 (Optimality Condition)

Theorem 218.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Given a microevolution algebra A over field F with invariance principle conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 64. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 238/5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 64 is immediate from the invariance principle property. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
